### [172] [Huber-Wasserstein barycenters for robust distribution-valued data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13131)
*Carlos Cardoso-Perelló,Alberto González-Sanz*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出将Huber损失直接嵌入最优传输代价，构造对分布值数据稳健的Huber-Wasserstein重心，它是Wasserstein均值与L1型Wasserstein中位数之间的自然插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有度量空间中的Huber均值是在Wasserstein距离优化之后对其施加Huber损失，只在“距离”层面进行稳健化；这种事后处理方式会损失位移层面的信息，且对局部形状污染不够稳健。作者希望直接在个体传输位移上施加Huber损失，以限制大位移的影响，同时保留近邻处的二次行为，从而获得兼具均值与中位数特性的稳健重心。

Method: 将Huber损失直接纳入最优传输代价函数，构造Huber-Wasserstein重心；证明Huber最优传输中对偶势元的正则性与唯一性、最优传输映射的存在性，以及随Huber参数变化的稳定性；对重心问题证明存在性与刻画结果、经验plug-in估计量的一致性及约1/2的有限样本崩溃点；在一维情形推导逐点影响函数与渐近分布，量化稳健性-效率权衡；通过受污染分布数据的数值实验检验稳健性及均值/中位数行为间的插值。

Result: 建立Huber-Wasserstein重心的分析与统计基础：得到Huber最优传输的对偶势正则性与唯一性、传输映射存在性及参数稳定性；重心存在且可刻画，经验估计量一致，崩溃点约等于1/2；一维下得到逐点影响函数和渐近分布并量化稳健性-效率权衡；证明位移式Huber化在局部形状污染下能保留距离式Huber化所丢失的一阶信息。

Conclusion: 在传输位移层面施加Huber损失所构造的重心，在保留局部二次行为的同时限制大位移影响，形成Wasserstein均值与L1型中位数的自然插值，兼具一阶信息的保留能力与约1/2的高崩溃点稳健性。

Abstract: We propose a robust barycenter for distribution-valued data by incorporating the Huber loss directly into the optimal transport cost. In contrast to metric-space Huber means, which apply the Huber loss to the Wasserstein distance after optimization, our construction acts on individual transport displacements, preserving quadratic behavior locally while limiting the influence of large displacements. The resulting Huber-Wasserstein barycenters form a natural interpolation between Wasserstein means and $L^1$-type Wasserstein medians.
  We establish the analytical and statistical foundations of this construction. For optimal transport with Huber loss, we prove regularity and uniqueness properties of dual potentials, existence of optimal transport maps, and stability as the Huber parameter varies. For the associated barycenter problem, we prove existence and characterization results, consistency of empirical plug-in estimators, and a finite-sample breakdown point essentially equal to $1/2$. In dimension one, we further derive the pointwise influence function and asymptotic distribution, quantify the associated robustness-efficiency trade-off, and show that displacement-wise Huberization can retain first-order information that is lost by distance-based Huberization under localized shape contamination. Numerical experiments on contaminated distribution-valued data demonstrate the robustness of the proposed barycenters and illustrate their interpolation between mean- and median-like behavior.

</details>


### [173] [R-estimation in a Linear Model with Autoregressive Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13150)
*Jana Jurečková,Hira Koul,Jan Picek*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在带自回归误差的线性回归模型中，基于自回归秩得分构造回归参数向量 β 的非参数估计，该估计对自回归参数不变，因而对潜在的隐藏线性趋势等结构化扰动不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济、水文等领域的数据常存在自回归误差或隐藏线性趋势等结构化扰动，传统估计方法容易受其干扰。作者希望构造一个对这类干扰（尤其是误差中的自回归成分）稳健的回归参数估计。

Method: 以模型中自回归秩得分（autoregression rank scores）为主要工具，构造回归参数向量 β 的非参数估计；该估计量对自回归参数具有不变性，从而免受误差自回归结构的影响。

Result: 所得估计量对自回归参数保持不变，能够抵抗误差中可能的自回归成分以及隐藏线性趋势等结构化扰动，适用于经济、水文等相关应用场景。

Conclusion: 通过自回归秩得分构造的非参数估计量，可有效消除自回归误差及结构化扰动对回归参数估计的干扰，为存在潜在隐藏趋势的线性回归问题提供了稳健的估计方案。

Abstract: In the linear regression model, we construct a nonparametric estimate of the regression parameter vector $\boldgreekβ$ that is insensitive to a possible nuisance autoregression in the model errors. The main tool for estimating $\boldgreekβ$ is based on the autoregression rank scores of the model. The resulting estimator is invariant to the autoregression parameters and thus remains insensitive to potential hidden linear trends or other structured disturbances, which frequently occur in economic, hydrological, and related applications.

</details>


### [174] [Chance-constrained selection of sequential intervention strategies from counterfactual estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13209)
*Minkyoung Kim,Beakcheol Jang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"预测-后优化"框架，在累计资源受限的情景下，通过机会约束从有限候选集中选择干预序列，以数据识别的方式约束成本超支的尾部概率，而非仅约束均值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多操作性决策是在累计资源限制下的一系列干预序列，需要同时估计每个候选策略的结果与累计成本（从观测数据中识别的反事实量）。现有两步法（含近期扩展到连续剂量的方法）只约束平均成本而非成本尾部，且只在单一时点分配；同一期望成本的两个策略其超预算的频率可能截然不同，约束均值无法约束超支发生概率。而能约束成本尾部的方法又依赖指定的参数模型，而非从数据中识别。因此需要一个从数据识别成本分布、并真正约束超支概率的决策框架。

Method: 提出"预测-后优化"（predict-then-optimize）框架。预测阶段：任何能返回结果值与其成本分布的估计器都能作为可互换的预测组件，供决策规则使用。优化阶段：在有限候选集上做机会约束选择，约束累计成本超过预算的概率不超过给定阈值。由于成本尾部无法跨阶段分解，因此对每个策略进行整体评分。通过扫描可容忍的违约概率，可描绘安全-效用前沿（safety-utility frontier），并给出免分布（distribution-free）的有限样本界，覆盖违约概率与结果损失（outcome shortfall）两方面。

Result: 在五个环境中验证，其中四个（涵盖临床治疗与设备维护）提供精确的反事实真值，第五个来自数字健康微随机化试验的真实结果。在所有环境中，该规则能在点估计规则会超预算的场合守住预算，而所付出的结果代价由安全-效用前沿明确呈现。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 所提出的机会约束预测-后优化框架能有效地从数据中识别成本分布并约束累计成本超支的尾部概率，在保证预算约束的同时，通过安全-效用前沿清晰量化与结果之间的权衡，并具备免分布的有限样本保证。

Abstract: Many operational decisions are sequences of interventions under a cumulative resource limit, such as a maintenance schedule within a crew-hour budget. Choosing among them calls for the outcome and the cumulative cost each would produce, counterfactual quantities identified from observational data. Two strategies with the same expected cost can exceed the budget at very different rates, so constraining the mean does not bound how often an overrun occurs. Prior two-step architectures, recently extended to continuous doses, constrain the mean cost rather than its tail and allocate at a single decision point. Methods that do bound a cost tail take its distribution from a specified model rather than identifying it from data. We present a predict-then-optimize framework. In the prediction step, any estimator returning an outcome value and a cost distribution supplies what the decision rule consumes, so the predictor is interchangeable. In the optimization step, a chance-constrained selection over a finite candidate set bounds the probability that the cumulative cost exceeds the budget. That tail does not decompose across stages, so each strategy is scored whole. Sweeping the tolerated violation probability traces a safety-utility frontier, and distribution-free finite-sample bounds cover violation and outcome shortfall. Four of five environments, spanning clinical treatment and equipment maintenance, supply exact counterfactual ground truth; the fifth carries real outcomes from a digital-health micro-randomized trial. Across them, the rule holds the budget where a point-estimate rule overruns it, at an outcome cost the frontier makes explicit. All code is available at https://github.com/mfriendly/counterfactual-chance-selection

</details>


### [175] [Causal Mediation Analysis for Network Data with Graph Neural Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13274)
*Peikai Wu,Zhiguo Xiao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在网络环境中提出非参数因果中介分析框架，同时处理中介与处理的溢出效应及高维网络混杂，用图神经网络估计混杂函数，建立估计量的渐近性质与方差估计，并在模拟和中国农村农业保险实验中验证方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果中介分析依赖无干扰假设，但网络化人群中该假设常被违反；现有方法难以同时刻画处理与中介的溢出、避免人为聚合模型，并应对高维网络混杂。

Method: 作者构建单个大型观测网络的非参数框架，用暴露映射和中介映射定义因果估计量以区分自身效应与溢出效应；在加强的条件独立条件下识别受控直接效应、自然直接效应和自然间接效应，并给出基于结构误差的原始充分条件；采用增广逆概率加权估计量（受控效应双重稳健、自然效应多重稳健），用图神经网络从节点特征和邻接矩阵中学习高维混杂函数；在近似邻域干扰、弱网络依赖及适当第一阶段收敛速率下，证明效应估计量的渐近正态性及网络HAC方差估计量的一致性。

Result: 模拟中图神经网络估计量优于基于手工构造邻域特征的机器学习方法；对中国农村农业保险实验的再分析发现保险知识是实质性中介渠道，而基于感知的中介变量不成立。

Conclusion: 所提框架能在放宽无干扰假设的网络数据中可靠识别并估计直接与间接因果效应，图神经网络有效处理高维混杂，为网络因果中介分析提供了理论与实证工具。

Abstract: Causal mediation analysis is typically formulated under no interference, an assumption often violated in networked populations. We develop a nonparametric framework for a single large observed network that allows simultaneous treatment and mediator spillovers and high-dimensional network confounding. Exposure and mediator mappings define causal estimands without restricting the true interference mechanism, separating own from spillover effects without prespecified aggregation models. Under strengthened conditional independence conditions, we identify own controlled direct, natural direct, and natural indirect effects and give primitive sufficient conditions in terms of structural errors. We construct augmented inverse probability weighted estimators that are doubly robust for controlled effects and multiply robust for natural effects, using graph neural networks to learn high-dimensional nuisance functions from node features and the adjacency matrix. Under approximate neighborhood interference, weak network dependence, and suitable first-stage rates, we establish asymptotic normality of the effect estimators and consistency of a network HAC variance estimator. In simulations the graph neural network estimator outperforms machine learning methods built on hand-constructed neighborhood features, and a reanalysis of an agricultural insurance experiment in rural China finds insurance knowledge to be a substantive mediating channel while perception-based mediators are not.

</details>


### [176] [Distributed Selective Inference for Quantile Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13311)
*Xiaohui Yuan,Jiahan Teng,Yan Zhou*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向高维分位数回归的分布式选择推断框架，通过响应替代策略将非光滑分位数损失转化为惩罚最小二乘问题，并结合随机化过程和KKT条件刻画Lasso选择事件，从而在仅三轮通信下实现有效的选择后推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维分位数回归中进行有效选择后推断面临两大挑战：一是分位数损失的非光滑性带来的计算困难，二是在分布式环境下如何保证推断的有效性并降低通信成本。

Method: 采用响应替代（response-surrogation）策略将分位数损失转化为惩罚最小二乘形式；引入随机化程序，通过Lasso选择的Karush-Kuhn-Tucker（KKT）条件刻画选择事件；在给定选择事件条件下推导聚合估计量的条件分布，并利用大偏差近似选择性似然以实现计算可处理；算法仅需本地机器与中心服务器间三轮通信。

Result: 在标准正则条件下建立了所提程序的渐近有效性，并通过大偏差近似使选择性似然计算可行。模拟研究与真实数据应用表明该方法具有良好的有限样本表现。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维分位数回归提供了有效的分布式选择后推断方案，兼顾了计算可行性与统计有效性，并通过模拟和实际数据验证了其优越性能。

Abstract: We propose a distributed selective inference framework tailored for high-dimensional quantile regression. To enable valid post-selection inference in this context, we address the computational challenge posed by the non-smooth quantile loss via a response-surrogation strategy. This strategy transforms the problem into a penalized least-squares formulation, thereby facilitating the application of distributed selective inference. For valid post-selection inference, a randomized procedure is introduced, in which the Lasso selection event is characterized through the associated Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions and the conditional distribution of the aggregated estimator is derived given the selection event. The resulting algorithm requires only three rounds of communication between local machines and the central server. Under standard regularity conditions, we establish the asymptotic validity of the proposed procedure and develop a large-deviation approximation to the selective likelihood for computationally tractable implementation. Simulation studies and a real-data application demonstrate the satisfactory finite-sample performance of the proposed method.

</details>


### [177] [Recursive Multiple Change Point Detection of Nonstationary Time Series: Instability Tests, Estimation and Confidence Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13352)
*Leheng Cai,Zhou Zhou*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出BARBS方法，一种基于高斯乘子bootstrap的递归二分分割法，用于非平稳时序下的多变点检测，具有稳健的误报控制、一致变点定位率与最优局部定位率，并在数值实验与美国通胀数据中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂的非平稳时间动态下，现有的多变点检测方法（如经典二分分割）在控制第一类错误、处理复杂相依结构以及变点定位精度方面存在不足，需要一种既能稳健控制误报、又能达到最优定位精度的递归检测方法。

Method: 提出bootstrap辅助的稳健二分分割（BARBS）方法：为CUSUM统计量构造新型高斯乘子bootstrap以应对复杂相依结构；在每个递归阶段精细校准临界值以确保原假设下控制第一类错误；在存在变点时以预设概率正确识别变点个数。进一步提出第二阶段精化估计器，用于获得最优个体定位率，并推导其在固定与消失跳跃幅度下的渐近分布及近最优一致定位率。

Result: BARBS在无变点时控制第一类错误；有变点时以预设概率正确识别变点数目，变点估计量达到与经典二分分割一致的一致收敛率；第二阶段精化估计器达到最优个体定位率，并具有固定与消失跳跃幅度下的渐近分布与近最优一致定位率。数值实验显示BARBS在各种设置下稳健且优于现有方法。

Conclusion: BARBS在复杂非平稳时序下实现稳健、高精度的多变点检测，兼具误报控制与最优定位精度；在美国通胀数据的应用中识别出的变点与多个已记录宏观经济事件吻合，验证了其实用价值。

Abstract: We develop bootstrap-assisted robust binary segmentation (BARBS), a recursive binary segmentation method for multiple change point detection under general nonstationary temporal dynamics. A novel Gaussian multiplier bootstrap for the CUSUM statistics is proposed, offering robustness to complex dependence structures. Through meticulous calibration of the critical values at each stage of the recursion, BARBS ensures control of the Type I error under the null hypothesis of no change points. When change points are present, BARBS identifies the correct number of changes with a prespecified probability, and the resulting change point location estimators attain the same uniform consistency rate as classical binary segmentation. Building on this, we introduce second-stage refined estimators that achieve the optimal individual localization rate, and establish their asymptotic distributions and nearly optimal uniform localization rates under both fixed and vanishing jump magnitudes. Extensive numerical experiments across various settings confirm the robustness and superior performance of BARBS relative to existing approaches. To illustrate the practical relevance of the proposed methodology, we analyze U.S. inflation data, yielding change points that align with several documented macroeconomic episodes.

</details>


### [178] [Weighted cumulative past inaccuracy and Kullback-Leibler divergence based on extropy: properties, estimation, and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13363)
*Bighneswar Sahoo,Suchandan Kayal*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于累积过去外熵（cumulative past extropy）的加权框架，用于度量两个非负寿命分布之间的差异，包括加权累积过去外熵非准确性（WCPEI）和加权累积过去外熵KL散度（WCPED），并拓展至动态情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的外熵（extropy）度量在刻画非负寿命分布差异时未充分考虑加权因素与充分利用过去时间区间的信息，因此需要发展加权框架以及动态（条件）版本，以更精准地度量分布间的不一致性和信息差异。

Method: 基于广义权重函数提出WCPEI与WCPED两类度量，并推导其理论性质；构造基于经验分布函数的非参数估计器（WCPEIR）；通过蒙特卡洛模拟研究有限样本表现；并进一步以给定时刻前可用的寿命信息为条件，提出动态度量DWCPEI与DWCPED，推导理论性质与非参数估计程序，用R软件仿真评估。

Result: 推导出各度量的理论性质，非参数估计器在不同样本量下的有限样本表现通过仿真验证；基于WCPED构建的外熵型拟合优度检验在不同备择假设下与多个已有均匀性检验比较了功效；图像分析应用中该度量能有效评估图像分辨率改变时像素强度分布的变化；动态度量的估计器有限样本性能良好。

Conclusion: 所提出的加权累积过去外熵框架（WCPEI、WCPED）及其动态扩展（DWCPEI、DWCPED）具备完善的理论基础与可行的非参数估计方法，在均匀性检验和图像分析等实际应用中表现出实用价值。

Abstract: This study develops a weighted framework for measuring the discrepancy between two nonnegative lifetime distributions through cumulative past extropy. We propose two measures, referred to as the weighted cumulative past extropy inaccuracy (WCPEI) and the weighted cumulative past extropy Kullback-Leibler divergence (WCPED). The generalized weight function is considered in this study. We investigate a number of theoretical properties of these measures. Empirical distribution function-based nonparametric estimator is subsequently constructed for the weighted cumulative past extropy inaccuracy ration (WCPEIR). Its finite-sample behavior is studied through Monte Carlo simulation experiments for different sample sizes. To illustrate the practical relevance of the WCPED, two applications are considered. First, an extropy-based goodness-of-fit procedure for testing uniformity is developed using the proposed divergence measure. Its power is then compared with that of several established uniformity tests under a variety of alternatives. Second, an image analysis application is presented in which the proposed measure is employed to assess changes in the distributions of pixel intensities when the image resolution is altered. The framework is further extended to a dynamic setting by conditioning on the lifetime information available up to a specified time point. This leads to the dynamic weighted cumulative past extropy inaccuracy (DWCPEI) and dynamic weighted cumulative past extropy divergence (DWCPED). Their theoretical properties are derived, and the corresponding nonparametric estimation procedures are proposed. The finite-sample performance of these estimators is evaluated through simulation studies using R software.

</details>


### [179] [COBRA-DOSE: Copula-based Bayesian Model Averaging for Dose Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13423)
*Luke Hagar,Min Zhang,Ranjeny Thomas,Andrew J. Martin*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出COBRA-DOSE框架，通过copula建模两个终点的依赖关系，并结合贝叶斯模型平均（Bayesian model averaging）处理边际分布与依赖结构的不确定性，用于早期临床试验中基于双终点的剂量选择，并通过风湿性关节炎I期试验DEN-181验证并在R的CobraDose包中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期剂量选择试验样本量小，传统方法只确定最大耐受剂量，而现代靶向治疗需寻找最佳生物学剂量（即达到足够生物学活性且安全性可接受的最低剂量）。在免疫学背景下，生物学活性需基于多个生物标志物或临床终点评估，临床医生需要透明的、跨剂量组合生物标志物结局概率的汇总；但小样本下复杂建模假设难以验证，故需一种不过度依赖单一模型的推断框架。

Method: 提出COBRA-DOSE框架：基于两个终点进行后验预测推断，用copula刻画两终点间的依赖关系，并通过贝叶斯模型平均同时考虑边际分布和依赖结构的不确定性，避免依赖单一模型，产出可解释的临床决策量。

Result: 以DEN-181（一项针对类风湿关节炎的I期免疫学试验）为例展示了COBRA-DOSE的性能，并在R中通过CobraDose包提供了该方法的通用实现。

Conclusion: COBRA-DOSE为小样本早期剂量试验提供了稳健、透明且可解释的双终点后验预测推断框架，有助于临床医生识别安全且有前景的剂量，并具有通用软件实现以保证其可应用性。

Abstract: Early-phase clinical trials for dose selection typically enrol few patients and aim to identify doses that are both safe and promising for further study. While traditional approaches identify the maximum tolerated dose, modern trials for targeted therapies often seek the optimal biological dose, defined as the lowest dose achieving sufficient biological activity with acceptable safety. In immunology settings, assessment of biological activity is based on multiple biomarkers or clinical endpoints. Clinicians leading dose-selection efforts would thus benefit from transparent summaries of the probabilities of observing combinations of biomarker outcomes across doses. However, such inference is challenging in small samples where complex modelling assumptions are difficult to verify. To address this limitation, we propose COBRA-DOSE, a framework for posterior predictive inference based on two endpoints that models dependence via copulas and accounts for uncertainty in both marginal distributions and dependence structures through Bayesian model averaging. This approach avoids reliance on a single model and yields interpretable quantities for clinical decision making. We demonstrate the performance of COBRA-DOSE using DEN-181, a phase I immunology trial in rheumatoid arthritis. We also provide a general implementation of our approach through the CobraDose package in R.

</details>


### [180] [Retrospective Statistical Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13439)
*Chong Gu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的统计推断范式：基于观测数据的点估计作为“事实”，通过模拟生成估计量的“克隆”，并从克隆分布中得出推断。该方法无需前瞻性有限维模型假设，也不对真实值作概率声明，适用于非参数函数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统统计推断依赖前瞻性有限维模型假设或对真实参数的概率声明，在非参数函数估计等复杂场景下难以操作；作者希望建立一种新的不确定性量化体系，无需此类假设即可进行推断。

Method: 以观测数据得到的点估计为中心，将该估计视为“真实值”，通过模拟生成重复样本（replicates），进而计算估计量的“克隆分布”（clone distribution），并从该分布出发进行推断；方法在平滑样条ANOVA模型的非参数回归中加以演示，亦适用于参数回归。

Result: 该范式在非参数函数估计中具备可操作性，并提供了一种替代性的不确定性量化系统；在参数回归中，所提出的推断在操作层面上与传统推断高度相似。

Conclusion: 这种以点估计为中心、通过克隆分布进行推断的新范式，能够在不依赖有限维模型假设、不对真实值作概率声明的前提下完成统计推断，展现了在非参数及参数回归中的广泛适用性与与传统方法的操作一致性。

Abstract: In this article, we explore a new paradigm for statistical inference. The approach centers around the point estimate based on observed data, simulating replicates using the estimate as the truth to produce clones of the estimate, with inference deriving from the clone distribution. It avoids prospective finite-dimensional model assumptions, but it makes no probabilistic claims concerning the truth; it suggests an alternative system of uncertainty quantification that is operable in nonparametric function estimation. The procedures are demonstrated using examples of smoothing spline ANOVA models in nonparametric regression. The paradigm also applies in parametric regression, where the proposed inference closely resembles traditional inference operation-wise. Conceptual discussions are scattered throughout.

</details>


### [181] [Theoretical Properties of Covariate-Adaptive Randomization with a Diverging Number of Covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13442)
*Yuhang Tao,Li-Xin Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在高维协变量（协变量数量发散）设定下，研究两类统一的协变量自适应随机化程序的理论性质，建立了指定协变量不平衡度量的收敛速率，并针对其中一类推导了未指定协变量不平衡的渐近性质，进而得到平均处理效应的差值均值估计量的渐近性质并构造渐近95%置信区间，同时辅以数值与实证研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代临床试验中实验者往往拥有大量协变量，而现有协变量自适应随机化理论多基于固定（有限）协变量维度，缺乏协变量数量发散情形下的理论支撑，因此需要建立高维设定下协变量自适应随机化程序的理论。

Method: 本文研究两类统一的协变量自适应随机化程序族，在协变量维度发散的高维设定下：1）建立与指定协变量对应的不平衡度量的收敛速率；2）对其中一类程序，研究未指定协变量不平衡的渐近性质；3）基于上述结果推导平均处理效应差值均值估计量的渐近分布并构造渐近95%置信区间；4）开展大量数值模拟与实证研究验证理论。

Result: 建立了高维设定下两类协变量自适应随机化程序指定协变量不平衡度量的收敛速率；对其中一类程序得到未指定协变量不平衡的渐近性质；据此给出平均处理效应差值均值估计量的渐近性质与渐近95%置信区间；数值与实证研究验证了理论结果的实用性。

Conclusion: 本文填补了协变量数量发散情形下协变量自适应随机化理论研究的空白，为高维协变量临床试验中的随机化设计与后续统计推断（平均处理效应估计与置信区间）提供了理论基础，并通过数值和实证研究证实了其实际应用价值。

Abstract: Covariate-adaptive randomization procedures are widely used in clinical trials to improve covariate balance. In modern applications, experimenters often have access to many covariates, motivating the need for a theory of covariate-adaptive randomization procedures with a diverging number of covariates. In this paper, we study the theoretical properties of two unified families of covariate-adaptive randomization procedures under high-dimensional settings. For the two procedures, we establish the convergence rate of the imbalance measure corresponding to the specified covariates. In addition, for one of them, we study the asymptotic properties of the imbalance of unspecified covariates under and apply these results to derive the asymptotic properties of the difference-in-means estimator for the average treatment effect and construct asymptotic 95% confidence intervals. Furthermore, we provide extensive numerical and empirical studies to illustrate the practical relevance of our theoretical results.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Black-Box Knowledge Transfer across Distinct Feature Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12403)
*Oh-Ran Kwon,Daeyoung Ham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从预训练黑盒预测函数向异构输入空间进行知识迁移的方法，通过分解目标回归函数为可迁移与不可迁移两部分，并利用特征对和无标签/少标签数据进行两阶段神经网络估计，从理论上证明其风险优于非迁移方法，并扩展到多黑盒聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练黑盒函数凝练了大规模数据与计算的预测知识，但当新输入空间的特征与黑盒期望的特征不一致时，无法直接使用黑盒。作者旨在解决这一异构输入空间下的知识迁移问题，使黑盒中的预测知识能被有效利用。

Method: 将目标回归函数分解为可迁移分量（可由黑盒提供信息）与不可迁移分量（捕捉新空间独有信息）。采用两阶段神经网络流程：第一阶段利用连接两个输入空间的大量无标签特征对估计可迁移分量；第二阶段利用有限标签估计不可迁移分量。从理论上推导了预测风险界，并将框架扩展到多个黑盒各处于自身输入空间的场景，通过聚合降低预测误差。

Result: 当不可迁移分量较小或平滑时，所提方法的预测风险界优于非迁移方法，且过程能自适应这两种情形。在附加条件下，估计量的最坏情形风险在多项式阶上严格小于仅用标签数据估计的极小极大风险。多黑盒聚合可相对最佳单一黑盒进一步降低预测误差。模拟与真实数据实验验证了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了黑盒预测知识向异构输入空间的有效迁移，理论上在不可迁移分量小或光滑时可获得优于非迁移方法的风险保障，并支持多黑盒信息聚合，兼顾理论最优性与实际可用性。

Abstract: Pre-trained black-box predictive functions encode knowledge distilled from massive datasets and extensive computation. However, when the available input features differ from those the black box expects, direct use is infeasible. We introduce a method for transferring predictive knowledge from the black box to a new, heterogeneous input space. Our approach decomposes the target regression function into a transferable component, which the black box can inform, and a non-transferable component, which captures information unique to the new space. We propose a two-step neural network procedure, estimating the transferable component from abundant unlabeled feature pairs that bridge the two input spaces and the non-transferable component from limited labels. We derive prediction risk bounds that improve on those of a non-transfer alternative when the non-transferable component is small or smooth, and the procedure adapts to either case. Under additional conditions, the worst-case risk of our estimator is of strictly smaller polynomial order than the minimax risk of estimation from the labeled data alone. We extend the framework to multiple black boxes, each on its own input space, and show that aggregation can reduce prediction error relative to the best single black box. Simulated and real data demonstrate the practical value of the method.

</details>


### [183] [SSPO: Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12443)
*Yuanyu Li,Jintao Xu,Zijiang Liu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Chen Xie,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 SSPO（结构感知相似度加权偏好优化），通过基于相异度的留一法基线对同批采样解联合打分，同时解决偏好优化中的梯度信号极化和均值基线中的基线冗余两大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经组合优化（NCO）训练依赖并行采样，但未能充分利用同一采样组中蕴含的丰富信息：偏好优化方法只锚定单一最优解，丢弃了其他解的细粒度质量与结构信号（梯度信号极化）；而基于均值的基线对所有解等权处理，导致结构近似的解以冗余信息淹没基线、梯度方差居高不下（基线冗余）。

Method: 提出 SSPO（Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization），对全部 B 个采样解通过相异度加权的留一法（leave-one-out）基线进行联合评分，使结构差异更大的解获得更高权重，从而在单一机制内同时化解两类失效。该基线的解嵌入为零参数、问题自适应，直接由编码器已有的节点表示构建。

Result: 在 TSP、EFL 和 JSP 基准上的实验表明，SSPO 相对此前的最优锚点基线与均匀权重基线均取得一致提升；在 TSP 和 EFL 上与均匀 RLOO 的直接对比证实，结构感知加权是性能提升的主要驱动因素。SSPO 训练的 EFL 策略已部署于京东的生产设施选址系统，验证了其在大规模场景下的实用性。

Conclusion: 结构感知的相异度加权留一法基线能同时克服梯度信号极化与基线冗余问题，显著提升 NCO 的训练效果，并且具备生产级可扩展性与实际部署价值。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) relies on parallel solution sampling for training, yet existing methods fail to fully exploit the rich information latent in a co-sampled solution group. Preference-optimization methods anchor on the single best solution and discard fine-grained quality and structural signal from all other peers-a failure we term gradient signal polarization. Mean-based baselines instead weight peers uniformly, so structurally near-identical peers flood the baseline with redundant information and keep gradient variance high-a failure we term baseline redundancy. We propose SSPO (Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization), which scores all $B$ sampled solutions jointly through a dissimilarity-weighted leave-one-out baseline: structurally distinct peers receive higher weight, resolving both failures in a single mechanism. The baseline uses zero-parameter, problem-adaptive solution embeddings built from the encoder's existing node representations. Experiments on TSP, EFL, and JSP benchmarks show consistent gains over prior best-anchor and uniform-weight baselines. A direct comparison against uniform RLOO on TSP and EFL confirms that structure-aware weighting is the primary driver of improvement. The SSPO-trained EFL policy has been deployed in a production facility-location system at JD$\mathord{.}$com, confirming practical viability at scale.

</details>


### [184] [Online Inference for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12973)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究分布强化学习中分位数时序差分学习（QTD）的统计推断问题。在生成模型假设下，证明了同步与异步QTD的函数中心极限定理（平均迭代弱收敛于重尺度布朗运动），并提出一种基于随机缩放的在线推断方法，构造渐近枢轴统计量，可在不存储整个迭代轨迹的情况下在线计算，大幅降低内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中，分位数时序差分学习（QTD）的统计推断缺乏严格的理论支撑与高效计算方法。现有方法通常需要存储完整迭代轨迹，内存开销大，难以实现高效的在线推断。本文旨在为QTD建立函数中心极限定理，并提供低内存、可在线计算的统计推断程序。

Method: 本文在可访问生成模型的假设下，首先为同步QTD和异步QTD建立函数中心极限定理，证明QTD的平均迭代弱收敛于重尺度布朗运动。随后提出基于随机缩放（random scaling）的在线推断方法：利用整条QTD路径的信息构造渐近枢轴统计量，该统计量可在线递推计算，无需存储整个QTD迭代轨迹。

Result: 同步与异步QTD的平均迭代均弱收敛于重尺度布朗运动，从而奠定了函数中心极限定理基础。所提随机缩放方法构造的统计量具有渐近枢轴性，并支持在线计算，显著降低了内存需求，实现了分布强化学习中高效的统计推断。

Conclusion: 本文为分布强化学习中的分位数时序差分学习提供了完整的统计推断框架：既建立了同步与异步情形下的函数中心极限定理，又通过随机缩放设计了内存高效、可在线计算的渐近枢轴推断方法，有效支撑了分布强化学习的统计推断实践。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to perform statistical inference for quantile temporal difference learning (QTD) in distributional reinforcement learning. Assuming access to a generative model, we first establish functional central limit theorems for both synchronous and asynchronous QTD, which show that the averaged iterates of QTD converge weakly to a rescaled Brownian motion. We next provide online inference methods. Based on random scaling, the inference procedure constructs an asymptotically pivotal statistic for inference by using the information along the whole QTD path. Meanwhile, the proposed statistic can be computed online without storing the entire trajectory of QTD iterates. This substantially reduces the memory requirement and enables efficient statistical inference in distributional reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [185] [Statistical Properties of Robust Learning under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13133)
*Zhiyi Li,Xiaojie Mao,Yunbei Xu,Ruohan Zhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究分布漂移下 DRO 与 Robust Satisficing 的有限样本泛化误差，推导目标环境中的误差界，提出信息导向的超参数校准，并在网络批量规划问题中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布漂移下 DRO 与 RS 的有限样本保证及其系统比较未被充分研究；现有分析要么基于源环境，要么只针对模糊集上的对抗最坏情况，缺乏对漂移后目标环境中泛化误差的刻画。

Method: 在漂移目标环境下同时推导 DRO 与 RS 的有限样本泛化误差界，刻画稳健超参数带来的"降低漂移敏感性"与"正则惩罚"之间的权衡；在给定漂移幅度或方向等部分信息时，提出信息导向的超参数校准方法并在相同信息下比较两方法；将框架应用于网络批量规划问题。

Result: 所得误差界显式刻画了漂移敏感性与正则惩罚的权衡，并避免了 Wasserstein 经验集中带来的维度灾难；在部分信息校准下，DRO 与 RS 在理论与经验上呈现互补的行为。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了分布漂移下稳健学习方法统计性质的理解空白，并为比较 DRO 与 RS 提供了原则性基础。

Abstract: Distributional shifts arise when the target deployment environment differs from the source environment that generated the training data. Robust learning frameworks such as Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) and Robust Satisficing (RS) aim to address this challenge, yet their finite-sample guarantees under such shifts, and their systematic comparison, remain underexplored: existing analyses typically establish guarantees either in the source environment or for adversarial worst-case performance over an ambiguity set. This paper instead studies generalization error in the target environment---the excess loss under the shifted target distribution. Our contributions are threefold. First, we derive finite-sample generalization error bounds in the shifted target environment for both DRO and RS. These bounds explicitly characterize the trade-off between reduced sensitivity to shift and the regularization penalty induced by each method's robustness hyperparameter, and they avoid the curse of dimensionality associated with Wasserstein empirical concentration. Second, when partial shift information such as shift magnitude or direction is available, we propose information-directed hyperparameter calibrations and compare the two methods given the same information. Under these calibrations, and in the partial-information regimes we study, DRO and RS exhibit complementary theoretical and empirical behavior. Finally, we apply the framework to a network lot-sizing problem, using it to interpret how robust policies respond to positive shifts in the demand distribution. Together, these results fill a gap in understanding the statistical properties of robust learning methods under distributional shifts and provide a principled basis for comparing DRO and RS.

</details>


### [186] [High-dimensional networks and mean squared error for possibly misspecified models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13171)
*Lourens Waldorp*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究高维网络（参数远多于观测）中节点邻域选择问题，指出传统方法（Lasso、AIC、BIC）在变量多时会引入虚假边（高假阳性率），而最小描述长度（MDL）通过将模型空间体积纳入惩罚，能在模型正确或错误假定为线性两种情况下都实现正确或更保守的邻域选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为避免遗漏网络中重要变量及其连接，网络分析包含越来越多的变量，导致参数数量远超观测的高维情境。作者旨在解决高维设置下如何获得低假阳性率（保守）的节点邻域估计，并揭示传统模型选择方法在此情境下失效的问题。

Method: 将邻域估计表述为线性模型，区分两种情形：（i）真实模型为线性时邻域选择尚可工作；（ii）真实模型为非线性时需为高维度施加惩罚。作者分析岭参数对均方误差的影响，揭示其导致低测试方差从而产生大量边的邻域，并结合机器学习中的“双重下降”现象；提出在参数众多时必须在惩罚中纳入模型空间的体积，并证明基于最小描述长度（MDL）的方法可实现正确或更保守的邻域选择。

Result: 在真实模型为线性时邻域选择表现尚可；真实模型为非线性时需施加高维惩罚。岭参数通过低测试方差导致包含大量边的邻域。Lasso、AIC、BIC 等方法会产生虚假边（高假阳性率），而最小描述长度（MDL）在高维设置下，无论模型是否为线性，都能实现正确或更小的邻域选择（低假阳性率），文中给出了相应证明。

Conclusion: 在高维网络分析中，为了得到可靠的邻域选择，必须在惩罚项中计入模型空间的体积。传统方法容易产生高假阳性率，而最小描述长度通过将模型复杂度与模型空间体积结合，能够在模型正确或误设（误设为线性）两种情况下都保持低假阳性率，实现正确或保守的邻域估计，呼应了机器学习中双重下降对传统模型选择观念的颠覆。

Abstract: To avoid missing important variables and their connections in networks, more and more variables are included in network analysis. Here we show that in a setting with many more parameters than observations (high-dimensional) it is possible to get a conservative (i.e., low false positive rate) estimate of the neighbourhood for each node (which connections are in the network). A neighbourhood is often estimated with a linear model, and this leads to two interesting cases: (i) If the true model is linear, then neighbourhood selection work reasonably well, and (ii) if the true model is nonlinear, then neighbourhood selection requires a penalty for the high dimensions. Here we show the impact of the ridge parameter on the mean squared error, and how this leads to low test variance and hence to neighbourhoods with large numbers of edges. We connect these insights with results from machine learning, where the so-called double descent (when more parameters are included than observations, the mean squared error goes down a second time) has put the traditional view on model selection upside down. Essentially, for adequate neighbourhood selection in models with a large number of parameters, the volume of the model space needs to be included in the penalty. Most neighbourhood selection methods (e.g., Lasso, AIC, BIC) lead to spurious edges (high false positive rate), but we prove that in the high-dimensional setting, minimum description length leads to correct neighbourhood selection or smaller (low false positive rates) in both cases when either the model is correctly or incorrectly assumed linear

</details>


### [187] [Sinkhorn Linearization and the Spectral Proxy: Unifying the Statistical and Algorithmic Theory of Feature-Parameterized Inverse Optimal Transport via a Single Spectral Sandwich](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13201)
*Han Dong,Jiaming Li,Yongqiang Gong,Ruixi Li,Yin Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文发展特征参数化代价下的逆最优输运（IOT）统计与算法理论，核心是Sinkhorn线性化及其谱代理，并据此建立可辨识性、稀疏一致性、适定性、收敛性与误指定五项结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优输运需要从观测的输运计划反演未知代价函数的参数，但缺乏统一的理论框架来刻画其统计可辨识性、估计的稀疏结构与算法保证。本文旨在以Sinkhorn线性化作为核心技术工具，建立一套从谱界限到统计与算法性质的完整理论。

Method: 考虑特征参数化代价 C_theta(i,j) = -theta^T phi(i,j)，核心提出Sinkhorn线性化——即熵正则最优输运计划对代价的隐函数敏感性，并给出其谱代理（spectral proxy），在切空间上受限Hessian满足谱夹层不等式 (pi_min/epsilon)I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon)I，从而导出核心下界 sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma))。在此核心界上建立四条定理（T1-T4）与一条观测（O5），涉及可辨识性、l1惩罚稀疏一致性、特征矩映射的强单调性、梯度下降局部强凸收敛性，以及误指定下的OT模型投影连续性。

Result: T1：theta在规范核的商空间上全局单射，维度界 F <= (K-1)^2；T2：l1惩罚估计量在不可表示性与得分集中条件下以指数级失败概率恢复真实支撑；T3：特征矩映射 M(theta)=Phi^T x_theta 强单调，其逆Lipschitz，常数 L <= epsilon ||Phi^T S_a||_op / (pi_min lambda_min(Sigma))；T4：局部强凸性 mu >= pi_min^2 lambda_min(Sigma)/epsilon^2 保证单调梯度下降收敛；O5：误指定估计量收敛到OT模型投影真值，投影映射的Holder连续性经数值评估，得到设定依赖的经验指数 alpha_eff 位于 (0,1)。

Conclusion: 本文通过Sinkhorn线性化建立统一的谱分析框架，从单一核心谱界推导出逆最优输运的可辨识性维度上界、稀疏估计一致性、适定性Lipschitz常数、算法局部强凸率及误指定投影连续性，系统刻画了特征参数化IOT的统计与算法理论基础。

Abstract: We develop the statistical and algorithmic theory of inverse optimal transport (IOT) under the feature-parameterized cost C_theta(i,j) = -theta^T phi(i,j). The core technical contribution is the Sinkhorn linearization -- the implicit-function sensitivity of the entropic OT plan to the cost -- together with its spectral proxy, a formula that is spectrally exact yet geometrically transparent.
  The restricted Hessian on the tangent space satisfies the spectral sandwich (pi_min/epsilon) I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon) I, yielding the single core bound sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma)) that drives the entire theory. On this core we establish four theorems and one observation.
  T1 (identifiability): theta is globally injective on the quotient of the gauge kernel, with dimension bound F <= (K-1)^2. T2 (sparsistency): the l1-penalized estimator recovers the true support under irrepresentability and score concentration, with exponential failure probability. T3 (well-posedness): the feature-moment map M(theta) = Phi^T x_theta is strongly monotone, and the inverse is Lipschitz with constant L <= epsilon ||Phi^T S_a||_op / (pi_min lambda_min(Sigma)). T4 (convergence): local strong convexity with mu >= pi_min^2 lambda_min(Sigma) / epsilon^2 guarantees monotone gradient descent convergence. O5 (misspecification): the estimator converges to the OT-model projection of the truth; the Holder continuity of the projection map is assessed numerically, yielding setting-dependent empirical exponents alpha_eff in (0,1).

</details>


### [188] [Wasserstein Filtering: A Sample Selection Method for Robust Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13418)
*Yikai Xu,Zhao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 Wasserstein Filtering (WF)，一种通过丢弃可疑样本、以剩余样本经验测度估计干净分布的样本选择框架。其核心思想是选出与全污染经验分布 Wasserstein 距离最大的子集，从而隔离几何上影响大的离群点。文中给出三种算法（SinkMarg、SinkWF、SlicedWF），并引入 FELP 污染模型证明 WF 估计量在有限协方差分布族上达到 minimax 最优，实验显示其在异常检测与扩散生成建模中均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际应用中，数据集中有一部分样本被污染，直接使用经验分布会严重偏离真实的干净总体分布。现有方法往往依赖特定模型或假设，缺乏一种模型无关、可扩展且具有理论保障的通用预处理手段来恢复底层干净分布。

Method: 作者提出 Wasserstein Filtering 框架：选取经验分布与全污染经验分布 Wasserstein 距离最大的子集，据此剔除几何上影响显著的离群点。为高效求解，提出三种算法——边际筛选方案 SinkMarg，基于熵正则最优传输的 SinkWF，以及基于切片 Wasserstein 近似的 SlicedWF。理论上引入 Far Exclusion and Local Projection (FELP) 污染模型，刻画远分离离群点与局部不可区分扰动两类污染。

Result: 在 FELP 模型下，证明 WF 估计量在具有有界协方差的分布族上达到 minimax 最优。在合成数据集、基准异常检测套件以及带重度污染的扩散模型鲁棒生成学习实验中，WF 均展现出竞争性的离群点检测性能，并在下游生成任务上带来显著收益。

Conclusion: Wasserstein Filtering 是一种实用、模型无关的预处理工具，能够有效从受污染数据中恢复干净分布，兼具理论最优性与计算可扩展性，并对异常检测和生成建模等下游任务具有明显帮助。

Abstract: Given a dataset where a portion of the samples are contaminated, our goal is to recover the underlying clean population distribution. To this end, we propose Wasserstein Filtering (WF), a novel sample selection framework that discards a fraction of suspicious samples and estimates the target distribution using the empirical measure of the remaining data. The core insight is to select a subset of samples whose empirical distribution maximizes its Wasserstein distance to the fully contaminated empirical distribution, thereby preferentially isolating and removing geometrically influential outliers. To render this optimization computationally tractable, we introduce three algorithms: a marginal screening scheme, SinkMarg, and two joint optimization algorithms, SinkWF and SlicedWF, leveraging entropic optimal transport and sliced Wasserstein approximations, respectively. On the theoretical front, we introduce the Far Exclusion and Local Projection (FELP) contamination model, which characterizes corruptions consisting of well-separated outliers and locally indistinguishable perturbations. Under this model, we prove that the WF estimator achieves minimax optimality over distribution families with bounded covariance. Extensive numerical experiments on synthetic datasets, benchmark anomaly detection suites, and robust generative learning with diffusion models demonstrate that WF serves as a highly practical, model-agnostic preprocessing tool. It delivers competitive outlier detection performance and provides substantial downstream benefits for generative modeling under heavy contamination.

</details>


### [189] [Bagging Robustly Learns VC Classes with Linear Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13514)
*Omar Montasser*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文重新研究测试时对抗扰动的鲁棒学习问题，证明 VC 类可以以线性于 VC 维 d 的样本复杂度实现对抗鲁棒可学习，将 Montasser、Hanneke 和 Srebro（2019）的先前上界指数级地改进。该结果通过将经典 bagging 启发式与鲁棒经验风险最小化（RERM）结合的简单非固有（improper）算法实现，并补充了下界证明该对偶 VC 维度依赖不可避免。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗样本鲁棒学习是机器学习中的重要难题，此前 Montasser 等人（2019）给出的上界相对于 VC 维度呈指数级，样本复杂度过高。作者希望探究能否显著改进这一上界，并理解 oracle 模型中调用 RERM 的固有成本下限。

Method: 提出一种简单的非固有（improper）算法：将 Breiman（1996）的经典 bagging（自助聚合）启发式与鲁棒经验风险最小化（RERM）结合。算法在 O(d*) 个独立自助样本（bootstrap samples）上分别计算 RERM，然后输出它们的多数投票（majority vote），其中 d* 表示对偶 VC 维度。同时构造下界以证明该依赖的必然性。

Result: 证明 VC 类可以被对抗鲁棒地学习，样本复杂度线性于 VC 维度 d，相比 Montasser、Hanneke 和 Srebro（2019）的上界实现了指数级改进。下界表明：一般情况下，该 oracle 模型中的任何学习器即使获得任意多的训练样本，也至少需要 Ω(d*) 次 RERM oracle 调用。

Conclusion: 通过 bagging 与 RERM 的简单结合即可实现对 VC 类的高效对抗鲁棒学习，且算法对 RERM oracle 的调用次数在对偶 VC 维度依赖上是渐近最优的（Ω(d*) 下界匹配）。这一结果既大幅改进了样本复杂度的理论界限，也揭示了 oracle 调用成本的下限。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of learning predictors robust to adversarial examples at test-time. We prove that VC classes are adversarially robustly learnable with sample complexity linear in the VC dimension $d$, providing an exponential improvement over the previous upper bound of Montasser, Hanneke, and Srebro (2019). Remarkably, this result is achieved with a simple improper algorithm that combines the classic heuristic bagging (bootstrap aggregation) of Breiman (1996) with robust empirical risk minimization (RERM). Our algorithm computes RERMs on $O(d^\star)$ independent bootstrap samples and outputs their majority vote, where $d^\star$ denotes the dual VC dimension. We complement this result with a lower bound showing that this is unavoidable: in general, any learner in this oracle model requires $Ω(d^\star)$ calls to an RERM oracle, even when given arbitrarily many training examples.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场性（position）论文，指出当前生成式AI对“推理”缺乏统一的运作化定义，导致推理评估的构念效度不可验证；作者通过综合文献提出“有效且可靠的推理是可学习的基于规则的过程”这一运作化定义，并给出AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI社区对“自主推理”的定义存在模糊性，且往往隐含地拒绝逻辑与可验证自动推理领域的经典处理方式。这种定义模糊使推理评估的构念效度无法验证，进而削弱了面向可信自主推理的可量化进展。作者希望证明这一模糊性是可以被解决的。

Method: 作者采用文献综合的方法，从逻辑学与自动推理等历史视角出发，对“推理”进行概念梳理与整合，提出运作化（operational）定义，将有效（valid）且可靠（sound）的推理定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的过程；同时制定一份用于AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践检查清单。

Result: 论文给出了清晰的运作化定义，将有效且可靠的推理界定为可学习的基于规则的过程，并提供了一份可供研究者参照的沟通最佳实践清单，从而为验证推理评估的构念效度提供了可操作的基础。

Conclusion: 定义模糊是当前AI推理研究乃至整个生成式AI领域的一个关键问题，但它是可以被解决的。通过明确运作化定义与最佳实践，研究者可以重新获得对推理评估构念效度的可验证性，从而推动可信赖的自主推理研究。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [191] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 IntegrityBench 基准，在 5 级隐式-显式压力协议下评估语言模型在科研操守方面的表现，发现前沿模型在高压下约有 1/3 的操守关键决策失败，且规模与推理能力无法可靠缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型正日益被用作"共同科学家"（co-scientists），但它们在机构压力下能否坚守科研诚信尚未得到测量，存在被利用从事科研不端行为或损害 AI 辅助科研信任的潜在风险。

Method: 构建 IntegrityBench 基准，包含 36 对配对任务，从三类能力（不端行为分类、伦理行动推理、基于证据/工件的决策）进行评估；采用横跨 3 个领域、4 个科研阶段、5 级隐式-显式压力协议施加不同程度的情境压力，并对 18 个前沿模型变体进行评测。

Result: 在最高压力下，模型约在 1/3 的操守关键决策中失败，且扩大规模或增强推理能力均无法可靠降低失败率；显式压力诱导模型顺从科研不端行为，而隐式情境重构更常导致对合法科研任务的过度拒绝。有趣的是，未能准确分类科研请求的模型在基于工件的决策上表现相当甚至更好（85.7 对 79.4），表明三种能力在结构上相互解耦，正确的伦理行动并不依赖准确分类。

Conclusion: 前沿模型可能表面上显得"有帮助"，却暗含操守缺陷，由此形成两类部署风险：一是助长科研不端行为，二是侵蚀人们对 AI 辅助科研的信任。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [192] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为现代AI对齐技术具有双重用途，既能防止有害输出，也可能被恶意行为者滥用为审查与操控工具，呼吁学界正视这一风险并制定缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI对齐方法本为抑制有害输出而生，但其技术机制天然具备可被反向滥用的双重用途潜力；尤其在AI迅速成为主要信息提供者、经济权力不对称加剧、政治环境趋向威权化的背景下，滥用风险被显著放大，亟需展开讨论。

Method: 本文为立场（观点）论文，采用概念映射与案例分析的方法：将现有对齐技术（如RLHF、价值观对齐、内容过滤等）与潜在的及已发生的滥用场景进行对应，论证"完美对齐"反而会赋予恶意行为者更强的信息支配能力。

Result: 论证表明，追求"完美对齐"的模型会不自觉地成为恶意行为者实施信息审查与操控的日益强大的工具，其双重用途风险正被用户快速采用、经济权力不对称与威权化政治趋势所加剧。

Conclusion: 作者敦促研究共同体正视AI对齐机制被刻意滥用的可能性，并建议制定缓解策略，以防范对齐技术的双重用途风险，防止其沦为信息控制的工具。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [193] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 评估 LLM 对齐不能只看最终标签是否与人类一致，还须考察其背后的道德理由。本文用 ETHICS 衍生的 500 项基准（覆盖五类道德判断），从标签和理由两个层面发现：模型与人类标签一致性高，但道德依据（对伤害、尊重、守诺、公正、应得、免责相关性的关注）存在系统性偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者指出，用“与人类判断一致”作为 LLM 对齐的代理指标存在隐患：最终标签一致并不代表模型与人类依赖相同的道德基础。两个智能体可以得出相同结论，却基于不同的原则、语境假设或情境解读。因此需要超越标签层面，检验模型在道德理由层面是否真正与人类对齐。

Method: 构建一个精选的 500 项 ETHICS 衍生基准，覆盖五类道德判断领域；为每项收集新的人类标注者与 LLM 的标注，既标注最终标签，也标注支撑理由（rationales）。随后对比人类多数标签与模型的标签一致性，并在理由层面对道德基础（如伤害、尊重、守诺、公正、应得、免责相关性等类别上的关注分布）进行系统性分析。

Result: 在前沿及开源模型家族中，模型与人类多数标签的一致性通常较高；但理由层分析显示，人类与模型所表达的道德依据出现系统性偏离。具体表现为：即使最终标签与人类多数一致，模型也会在伤害、尊重、守诺、公正、应得、免责相关性等类别之间重新分配关注度（redistribute attention）。

Conclusion: 一致性（agreement）不应等同于对齐（alignment）。仅基于标签的评估可能给出误导性的“安心”结论；评估必须补充对模型判断中所表达的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析，才能真正衡量 LLM 的道德对齐程度。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [194] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MAS-DecStream，核心贡献为 LLM-MR-CNP——对经典合同网协议（CNP）的扩展，引入语义化 CFP 表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证，用于异构移动边缘-云基础设施中的去中心化流处理调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统日益运行于异构移动边缘-云基础设施之上，面临工作负载波动、资源争用和严格 QoS 要求的挑战，使去中心化调度复杂化；现有方法难以同时处理定量硬约束与定性不确定运行时上下文。

Method: 提出 MAS-DecStream，核心为 LLM-MR-CNP 扩展：边缘集群智能体基于局部观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文，借助 LLM 生成并多轮细化自然语言卸载提案；硬资源与 QoS 约束保持确定性验证；引入语义 CFP 表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证机制。

Result: 在 Alibaba ASI Trace 派生的实验上，MAS-DecStream 将延迟违规降至 3%，消除资源过度承诺，20 个智能体下冲突解决率达 0.91，效用较多轮规则基线提升最多 22%；25 个案例的独立评估显示准确率-成本权衡依赖模型与提示设计。

Conclusion: 多轮 CNP 细化是协议层面的主要收益来源，LLM 辅助在定性与不确定运行时上下文场景中提供附加价值；结果提供了初始证据。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [195] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张在高风险决策等场景中，需要构建"认知对齐"的AI系统：即AI的推理方式与其用户相似，并能忠实传达推理过程；文章回顾证据、补充调查数据、指出差距并提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI日益被用作决策辅助、决策代理或自主决策者，但在高风险决策等许多场景中，若AI的推理方式与用户不一致且不能忠实沟通其推理，用户往往难以理解、信任并合理地依赖AI，从而阻碍AI的采纳。

Method: 本文采用立场性论述：回顾认知对齐有助于提升可理解性与可信度的已有证据；补充新的调查数据（显示当AI判断或行动的理由对用户重要时，许多用户认为认知对齐"至关重要"）；对比现有对齐方法与实现认知对齐所需之间的差距；并提出研究议程。

Result: 证据表明认知对齐能提升可理解性与可信度；新调查显示，当AI理由对用户重要时，许多用户将其视为"至关重要"。同时指出现有对齐方法无法充分实现认知对齐。

Conclusion: 认知错位是许多设想中AI应用被采纳的可能障碍；解决认知对齐问题对于构建用户既愿意又有理由依赖的AI系统至关重要。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [196] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型安全对齐行为的语言依赖性：在高风险博弈场景中，提示所用的语言会改变模型的决策。研究发现，日本语推理显著降低Claude和Gemini模型的"发射"（核打击）概率，且驱动因素是模型被要求推理所用的语言而非输入语言；其他五个模型无语言效应，但无论何种语言几乎都会发射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地用于战略与咨询场景，但其安全对齐通常仅在英语中进行评估。作者质疑：提示语言是否会在高风险场景中改变模型的决策，进而导致仅用英语评估会遗漏其他语言中编码的风险或安全机制。

Method: 作者测试了来自六家供应商的九个模型，采用单轮博弈论情景短文（vignette），让模型以顾问身份建议一个拥核国家是否打击一个无防御能力的对手。提示在跨语言间保持刻意非道德化（amoral）且在战略上完全一致。通过跨语言实验隔离机制：比较英语/日语提示，以及在英语提示中要求用日语推理，以区分"输入语言"与"推理语言"的作用。

Result: ① 日语提示降低Claude系列的发射率：Claude Sonnet 4.6在打击不必要场景中从40%降至0%，在有争议场景中从93%降至17%，而在战略理性打击场景中几乎无效。② 该效应延伸至Gemini Pro 3.1（53%降至13%）。③ 跨语言实验显示，在英语提示中指令用日语推理时，发射率从93%降至37%，说明是推理语言（而非输入语言）驱动该效应。④ 用日语推理时，模型会自发产生提示中完全不存在的道德词汇（如"道德代价""数百万生命"）。⑤ 其余五个模型无语言效应，但它们在几乎所有条件下都会发射。该效应要求模型本身已在英语中表现出犹豫。

Conclusion: LLM的安全行为具有语言依赖性，仅用英语评估会同时遗漏其他语言中编码的风险与安全防护；该现象在为模型的安全性与对齐鲁棒性评估时值得关注。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [197] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Dual-Flow Transformer，将推理的两个阶段（prefill 与 decode）解耦：主流程负责完整因果语言建模并写入 KV 缓存，辅助流程仅从最终提示位置起参与续写预测，不写持久状态。两条流程共享主要注意力、MLP、输出矩阵，但使用独立词嵌入与轻量耦合。该设计在匹配 token 比较中取得更低验证损失，并在 MoE 模型中实现 prefill/decode 专家扇出的独立控制，揭示 prefill-decode-质量之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型服务请求增多，累计推理成本相对于一次性训练成本愈发重要。prefill 是并行且通常计算受限，decode 是串行且通常内存带宽受限；传统的宽度或深度扩展会同时提高两阶段成本（每个新增层两阶段都评估）。因此希望将额外的学习计算专门分配给续写预测，同时保留 prompt 侧主计算与单一持久 KV 缓存。

Method: 提出 Dual-Flow Transformer：主流程是完整因果语言模型，处理 prompt 并写 KV 缓存；辅助流程在 prompt 处理时被省略，仅从最终 prompt 位置起激活，为续写预测增加计算而不写持久状态、不影响主流程。两条流程共享主要的注意力、MLP 与输出矩阵，使用独立 token 嵌入及轻量耦合。共享权重与主缓存还使分组执行时可复用已加载权重及缓存的 key/value。

Result: 在匹配 token 的比较中，Dual-Flow 在多种架构与数据配置下取得更低验证损失。在 MoE 模型中，分离设计使主/辅助专家扇出成为对 prompt 成本、续写成本与预测质量的独立控制；实验涵盖“在固定 prefill 专家计算下增加 decode 计算”与“在固定 decode 专家预算内于两流程间重新分配”两种机制，暴露了 prefill-decode-质量权衡，展示了相位特定专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer 通过将 prefill 与 decode 的计算解耦，在保持主流程 prompt 计算与单一持久 KV 缓存的同时，将额外学习计算专门投入续写预测，从而以更低的验证损失和更精细的 MoE 成本-质量权衡控制，优于统一的宽度/深度扩展方案，为相位特定的推理成本优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [198] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PAC-Bayes 正则化的 Meta-LoRA 方法，用于跨域零样本/少样本个性化对话生成，通过元学习初始化与证据量自适应校准更新幅度，并将个性化先验功能分解为用户（可读提示）与领域（软令牌）两部分，在多个基准上显著优于基线，尤其缓解了跨域胜率退化和冷启动问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域零/少样本个性化需要在稀疏的目标域交互数据下生成符合用户偏好的回复。现有适配方法在证据稀疏时难以校准更新幅度、容易过拟合；历史迁移方法则常将用户偏好与源域噪声产物纠缠在一起，导致先验不可靠和负迁移。因此需要一种既能根据证据质量校准适配强度，又能正确区分可迁移偏好与域相关表达的方法。

Method: 提出 PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA：以元学习得到的 LoRA 初始化同时作为适配起点和先验中心，并依据支持集大小与预测不确定性动态调整更新强度；证据稀疏或模糊时抑制过拟合，证据充足时增强个性化。同时将个性化先验功能分解为用户部分与领域部分——用人类可读提示承载稳定偏好，用保拓扑（topology-preserving）软令牌承载领域特定的隐空间条件控制。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上取得一致增益，强于基线。在 HiCUPID 上，跨域胜率退化相较最佳基线减少 47.9%，未见用户冷启动场景下胜率提升 110.2%。

Conclusion: 通过 PAC-Bayes 正则化校准 LoRA 适配强度，并将个性化先验分解为用户与领域组件，能有效缓解稀疏证据下的过拟合与源域负迁移，实现可靠的跨域零/少样本个性化，实验验证了方法的一致优越性。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [199] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍阿斯利康内部的 LLM 系统「Research Assistant」，它通过聊天界面整合文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全、表达数据及内部实验系统等多源生物医学证据，支持快速问答与多步研究两种模式，回复均基于检索证据并回链到原始来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阿斯利康的科学家和临床研究人员在日常研发中需要跨多个异构数据源探索生物医学问题，传统方式繁琐低效；因此需要一个基于 LLM 的统一助手，以自然语言高效整合并追溯多源证据，支撑日常 R&D 工作流。

Method: 构建内部 LLM 驱动的对话系统，汇聚科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据和内部实验系统等数据源；提供快速模式（直接问答）和多步模式（复杂研究任务）；采用检索证据落地（grounding）机制，使回复与检索到的证据关联并回链到原始来源；此外给出系统架构、关键产品设计决策与大规模部署的经验总结。

Result: 该系统在阿斯利康内部大规模部署，能够支持科学家和临床人员的日常 R&D 工作流，实现跨多源数据的生物医学问题探索，并通过证据回链保证回复的可信性和可追溯性。

Conclusion: Research Assistant 成功将 LLM 应用于制药研发场景，通过证据落地、多源整合和双模式交互提升了科研与临床探索效率；技术笔记总结了架构与设计选择，并分享了企业级规模化部署的实践教训。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [200] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束饱和评估（CSE）基准，系统研究大语言模型在多约束同时生效时的表现衰减，发现约束间近乎独立导致成功概率呈乘法式崩塌，并在5-6条约束时出现可靠指令遵循的崩溃点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被部署在需要同时满足多重显式约束的场景（推理结构、安全边界、输出模式等）。尽管单一约束已被良好处理，但多约束组合情形下的表现如何衰减、衰减由什么主导、能否缓解，仍缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 作者构建了约束饱和评估（CSE）基准：程序化生成任务，系统地改变同时施加的约束数量 k，所有约束均由确定性的规则验证器评分，完全无需 LLM 裁判参与。共覆盖 15 个模型、36 种约束类型、在 k=1 到 12 之间完成 369,753 次检查。

Result: 得出三个发现：1）单条约束通过率缓慢且可预测地衰减，但满足全部 k 条约束的概率崩塌——当 k=8 时，模型单条约束通过率约 41%，而八条全部通过的概率仅 5.7%。2）约束退化并不均等：结构性约束每新增一条约束损失的基础能力是词法性约束的 2 倍，这源于"理解-维持"差距，即需要持续追踪的约束比不受组合影响的二元决策退化更快。3）失败几乎相互独立，这正是累积呈乘法性的原因；残余的耦合源于共享输出特征而非两两干扰——比如错误的句数会使所有读取该特征的约束全部失败。可靠的指令遵循在超过 5-6 条同时约束后即崩溃：最强模型的探测级成功率在 7 条约束时跌破 50%，15 个模型中有 12 个在 3 条或更少约束时即已跌破。

Conclusion: 多约束组合下 LLM 的可靠指令遵循存在明确的容量极限：约束失败近独立导致成功概率乘法式崩塌，结构性/需持续追踪的约束退化更快，且残余耦合由共享输出特征驱动。这为理解和缓解组合约束下的性能下降提供了实证基础与方向。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [201] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MindMemOS，一个可移植、自进化的记忆操作系统层，采用统一的“实体-属性-时间”结构组织开放世界信息，并通过进化式搜索、记忆梦境整合、人类反馈纠正和技能演化等机制，使 AI 智能体的记忆与技能能够随使用持续优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统在开发完成后往往保持固定，无法在使用过程中自适应地调整其记忆模型、组织策略和过程性知识，限制了智能体在长期交互中积累经验、保持个性化与持续适应的能力。

Method: 提出 MindMemOS 记忆操作系统层，核心包括：1) 统一的实体-属性-时间结构组织开放世界信息；2) MindMemEvolve 算法，采用验证驱动的进化搜索优化目标场景的记忆模式，并通过“梦境”整合累积记忆（合并冗余记录、解决冲突）；3) 隐式纠正反馈作为人在环信号，识别并修正不准确或不一致的记忆；4) MindSkillEvolve 算法将智能体的执行轨迹转化为可复用、逐步精炼的技能。

Result: 在 LOCOMO 上取得 94.03% 的准确率，在 PersonaMem 上取得 70.63% 的准确率；MindSkillEvolve 在 SpreadsheetBench 上相较初始技能基线将成功率提升 9.2 个百分点。

Conclusion: MindMemOS 通过可移植、自进化的记忆组织与技能演化机制，实现了记忆模式的场景自适应、高阶模式发现、自主细化与持续技能进化，显著提升了智能体在长期交互中的记忆准确性和任务成功率，为构建可随使用成长的 AI 智能体提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [202] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可审计的治理式持久记忆模型（GPM），通过双时态状态转换、来源绑定准入、派生生命周期、公共屏障与故障关闭式结构化发布等机制，确保检索出的长期记忆记录在支持回答时不会引入矛盾、被取代、撤回或过期信息，并给出五个可执行条款及大规模基准验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期智能体记忆的"选择-存储-检索"范式只关注检索本身，却不判断被检索出的矛盾、被取代、撤回、删除或过期记录是否仍能支撑最终主张，导致不可信输出。

Method: 提出 GPM：一种可审计的双时态状态转换模型，包含来源绑定准入、派生生命周期状态、当前公共屏障及故障关闭式结构化发布，并以五条可执行条款约束账本完整性、来源绑定、冲突隔离、撤回/删除后不复活，以及在单一已验证头部的全新视图上的精确主张闭包。验证分为预设的哈希冻结 3,600 案例基准、密封端到端服务评估（V3、V5 两轮）、生产代码无关的有限模型穷举（331,776 语义状态与 1,990,656 查询状态）及 100,000 轨迹三引擎差异化比对。

Result: 在 GPM-ReleaseBench 上，GPM 匹配全部完整结果；三种最简完整策略中最强者仅得 1,800/3,600，且在 50% 的违规案例上产生不应有的发布。密闭服务评估中 V3 臂治理通道在 2,400/2,400 簇上全部正确，而无治理本地 Qwen2.5-7B 为 600/2,400；修复全部 1,800 个基线失败且零回归（单侧 95% 下界 99.875% 与 99.834%）。V5 复封（中英文指令臂、生成日期固定、冻结后不改归约器）每臂再次获得 2,400/2,400。有限模型无完整契约反例，三引擎差异化比对零失配。

Conclusion: 这些结果是对契约与实现的有界验证，而非开放世界模型精度或世界真值的证据；治理问答在密封服务评估中为确定性服务输出，7B 模型的对比结果只是无治理对照，不代表语言模型本身变得完全精确。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is usually treated as select--store--retrieve, but retrieval does not decide whether contradictory, superseded, retracted, deleted, or stale records may support an outgoing claim. We introduce Governed Persistent Memory (GPM), an auditable bitemporal state-transition model with source-bound admission, derived lifecycle state, current public barriers, and fail-closed structured release. Five executable clauses cover ledger integrity, source binding, conflict isolation, non-revival after retraction or deletion, and exact claim closure over a fresh view at one verified head.
  On a prespecified hash-frozen 3,600-case GPM-ReleaseBench, GPM matches all complete outcomes; the strongest of three intentionally simple complete policies matches 1,800/3,600 and makes unmatched releases on 50% of violation cases. A separate sealed end-to-end service evaluation exercises real ingestion and release across eight query families. In its publicly disclosed V3 arm, the governed lane is correct on 2,400/2,400 clusters versus 600/2,400 for ungoverned local Qwen2.5-7B; it repairs all 1,800 baseline failures with no regression (one-sided 95% lower bounds 99.875% and 99.834%). A later V5 reseal over Chinese- and English-command arms, with generation-date pinning and no post-freeze reducer amendment, again obtains 2,400/2,400 per arm. A production-code-independent finite model explores 331,776 semantic and 1,990,656 query states without a full-contract counterexample, and a 100,000-trace three-engine differential yields zero mismatches.
  These are bounded contract and implementation results, not open-world model accuracy or evidence of world truth. Governed answers in the sealed service evaluation are deterministic service outputs; the 7B result is the ungoverned comparison, not a claim that a language model itself became perfectly accurate.

</details>


### [203] [$\varepsilon$-MemEvo: Adaptive Cross-Task Memory Transfer for LLM Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12522)
*Aofan Liu,Shiyuan Song,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 ε-MemEvo 框架，将 LLM 程序进化（如 FunSearch、AlphaEvolve）中的历史搜索经验以"任务无关的战术记忆"形式存储，并通过自适应注入门实现跨任务知识迁移，在 8 个优化基准上相对 AdaEvolve 平均提升 AUCC +8.7%、早期收敛 +9.4%，且计算开销不足 1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 程序进化系统（FunSearch、AlphaEvolve）虽能发现新颖算法，但往往针对每个任务孤立优化，任务完成后即丢弃搜索经验，导致无法复用既有知识；同时跨任务迁移面临 API 与评估器不同、语义不匹配引发负迁移等挑战。

Method: 提出 ε-MemEvo 框架：(1) 将先前经验存储为任务无关的"战术记忆"——即对成功算法策略的紧凑自然语言摘要，而非原始代码，以支持跨不同 API/评估器的任务迁移；(2) 设计自适应注入门（adaptive injection gate），动态决定是否注入检索到的记忆以及注入强度，以避免语义不匹配记忆带来的负迁移。评估采用 8 个多样化优化基准（涵盖数学优化与系统工程），并使用内容级 Leave-One-Out 协议排除目标任务自身的记忆条目。

Result: 在主骨架 GPT-5 上，ε-MemEvo 在全部 8 个任务上相对 AdaEvolve 提升 AUCC，平均相对增益 +8.7%，早期收敛平均提升 +9.4%。消融实验表明：朴素记忆注入可能灾难性失败，而自适应门控在全部 5 个消融任务上保持安全。数据更新后的后验在观测状态下可解释：在改进型搜索期间倾向"跳过"，并在早期与晚期平台期由"跳过"转向"提示"。所有增益的计算开销低于 1%。

Conclusion: ε-MemEvo 验证了将历史搜索经验以任务无关战术记忆形式跨任务复用、并辅以自适应门控防止负迁移的有效性，能以极低计算开销稳健提升 LLM 程序进化系统在多种优化任务上的性能与早期收敛速度。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution systems such as FunSearch and AlphaEvolve have shown strong ability to discover novel algorithms, but typically optimize each task in isolation, discarding search experience after completion. We introduce $\varepsilon$-MemEvo, a framework for cross-task knowledge transfer in LLM program evolution. $\varepsilon$-MemEvo stores prior experience as task-agnostic tactic memories: compact natural-language summaries of successful algorithmic strategies rather than raw code, enabling transfer across tasks with different APIs and evaluators. To avoid negative transfer from semantically mismatched memories, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo uses an adaptive injection gate that decides whether retrieved memories should be injected, and at what intensity. We evaluate $\varepsilon$-MemEvo on 8 diverse optimization benchmarks spanning mathematical optimization and systems engineering, using a content-level Leave-One-Out protocol that excludes target-task memory entries. On the primary GPT-5 backbone, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo improves AUCC over AdaEvolve on all 8 tasks, with a mean relative gain of +8.7%, and improves early-stage convergence by +9.4% on average. Ablations show that naive memory injection can fail catastrophically, while adaptive gating remains safe across all five ablation tasks. The data-updated posterior is interpretable in observed states: it favors skip during improving search and shifts from skip to hint across early and late plateaus. These gains incur less than 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [204] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出因果归因分数（CAS）架构，将干预效应分解为基于因果Shapley的局部与全局解释，并将其与纯预测性解释区分开来。模拟与实证（401(k)、宾州再就业奖金）表明，CAS能更准确地捕捉干预效应的异质性，优于一次性归一化和全局绝对ATE等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预测性解释方法（如SHAP）只归因模型输出，而非真实世界干预效应；它们无法回答“哪些特征解释了干预效应的异质性”。作者希望建立一套以显式干预目标为核心的局部到全局因果解释层，将“预测结果”与“解释因果效应异质性”区分开。

Method: CAS架构从已识别的干预性联盟博弈出发，用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，再将原始结果尺度效应转换为Local CAS、Signed Local CAS以及两个互补的Global CAS汇总。创新点不在于新Shapley公式，而在于带显式干预目标的局部到全局因果报告层（含联盟感知归一化）。

Result: 已知真值基准中，8次重复主交互模拟（每次n=2,200，三个动作）显示联盟感知CAS的平均局部CAS MAE为0.107，优于一次性归一化的0.173和全局归一化绝对ATE向量的0.213；相对一次性归一化的优势从可加性下的-0.003提升到强交互下的0.091。在两个DoubleML实证数据集（401(k)，n=9,915；宾州再就业奖金，n=5,099）上，预测性SHAP/TreeSHAP排序与Feature-CAS对处理效应调节变量的排序存在实质差异；宾州数据中dep1（恰好一名受扶养人）从预测性全局第13名升至Feature-CAS第2名，并成为最主要的局部Feature-CAS调节变量。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了将“预测结果的因素”与“解释估计因果效应异质性的因素”区分开来的附加价值；CAS通过显式干预目标与联盟感知归一化，提供了更准确的干预效应因果解释。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [205] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 trie automaton（前缀树自动机），一种针对“从有限字符串集合中生成合规输出”这一约束解码场景的专用机制，通过 Aho-Corasick 多模式匹配预计算每个节点的 token 掩码，突破现有通用文法编译在值数量上千时遇到的“基数墙”，实现更快的编译与每步计算，并在批处理服务中带来 29 倍端到端吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益需要生成符合预定义 schema 的结构化输出，其中常见约束是从有限合法字符串集合中选择。现有约束解码系统依赖通用文法编译，当合法值数量增长到数千时编译与每步计算变得极慢（即“基数墙”），成为瓶颈。

Method: 作者设计并实现了 trie automaton：利用有限集合的结构特性（共享前缀、有界深度、已知基数），结合 Aho-Corasick 多模式匹配算法，预计算每个 trie 节点的有效 token 掩码。预计算掩码支持无状态（stateless）服务路径，可绕过 guided decoding 流水线，从而在批处理服务中进一步释放性能。

Result: 与主流后端 XGrammar 相比，trie 的每步有效 token 计算快 7 倍（0.65 us vs. 5.8 us），在 K>=300 时编译快 2–6.5 倍。在 batch size 256 下端到端 vLLM 吞吐达 219 req/s，而 XGrammar 为 7.5 req/s（29 倍提升）。在七个 tokenizer 家族（词表 32K–262K）上，trie 在 K=10,000 时编译仍低于 100ms，且每步开销不随集合大小增长，同时保证 100% 输出合法性。

Conclusion: trie automaton 通过针对有限集合结构的专用预计算掩码方法，有效绕开了通用文法编译的基数墙，显著提升了约束解码的编译速度、每步计算效率及批处理吞吐，并保证输出完全合法，为大规模合规结构化生成提供了高效方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [206] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"推理陪审团"（Reasoning Jury）系统，用多个 LLM 组成的陪审团配合主持人和共识机制，替代单一模型裁判，以更准确地识别长推理轨迹中的推理缺陷，且成本远低于前沿模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评判长推理轨迹的质量对于推理 LLM 的数据筛选、强化学习训练信号和性能评估至关重要，同时发现推理错误还能在运行时反馈以改进模型。然而，单一模型裁判（即使是前沿模型）难以准确识别长推理轨迹中的推理缺陷，且前沿模型因使用条款限制通常不能用于推理 LLM 的在线训练。

Method: 提出 Reasoning Jury 系统，以 LLM 陪审团和受主持的共识机制取代单一裁判。系统通过"审议"（deliberation）过程浮现推理轨迹的缺陷及其严重程度：主持人（moderator）引导陪审团成员（jurors）展开讨论，陪审员相互批判彼此的判断并可修改初始投票，主持人最终通过陪审员之间的审议或对判断的整合（consolidation）得出共识。

Result: 使用开放权重模型（如 gpt-oss-120b）组成的推理陪审团，在正确识别推理缺陷上显著优于前沿模型（opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6、gemini-3.1-pro）。除准确率提升外，陪审团的总成本（初始裁决、审议、整合等）仅为前沿模型 LLM-as-a-judge 方案成本的 8 至 15%。

Conclusion: 通过多模型陪审团与审议式共识机制，可低成本地大幅提升长推理轨迹缺陷识别的保真度；这些判断还可用于深入理解推理 LLM 在基准测试上的失败模式，从而更深刻地评估模型性能。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation.
